        Idea:
Implement Partner Bridge paths while preserving existing bridge functionality and existing local distillation edits. Add a partner bridge path field to existing bridge candidates with values message_partner, coach_in_person, casual_share, hold_for_context, ask_permission, do_not_bridge. message_partner means do not proactively send now; it means keep the ready bridge in the target partner prompt/hot context until it is substantively addressed. Keep send_bridge_candidate as explicit immediate send behavior but clarify prompt semantics. Hot context should surface ready/actionable target-facing partner bridges only, support multiple ready bridges, and exclude sent/addressed by default. Prompt should use Partner Bridge terminology, resolve the escalate_to_partner conflict, require marking addressed only when substantively dealt with or target declines/no longer wants to discuss, and instruct continued discussion until addressed. Add schema, migration, tool, hot context, prompt, and focused tests. Consider whether existing memories/observations/distillations need backfill: do not auto-create historical bridges in migration; document/manual/script path if needed.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/migrations/0016_partner_bridge_paths.sql\n+BEGIN;\n+\n+-- Historical bridge_candidates rows default to message_partner. This migration\n+-- does not auto-create or infer historical partner bridges; any reclassification\n+-- is manual or script-based.\n+ALTER TABLE bridge_candidates\n+    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS partner_path text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'message_partner';\n+\n+ALTER TABLE bridge_candidates\n+    ALTER COLUMN partner_path SET DEFAULT 'message_partner';\n+\n+UPDATE bridge_candidates\n+SET partner_path = 'message_partner'\n+WHERE partner_path IS NULL;\n+\n+ALTER TABLE bridge_candidates\n+    ALTER COLUMN partner_path SET NOT NULL;\n+\n+DO $$\n+BEGIN\n+    ALTER TABLE bridge_candidates\n+        ADD CONSTRAINT bridge_candidates_partner_path_check\n+        CHECK (partner_path IN (\n+            'message_partner',\n+            'coach_in_person',\n+            'casual_share',\n+            'hold_for_context',\n+            'ask_permission',\n+            'do_not_bridge'\n+        ));\n+EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;\n+END $$;\n+\n+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bridge_candidates_target_ready_message_partner\n+    ON bridge_candidates (target_user_id, created_at DESC)\n+    WHERE status = 'ready' AND partner_path = 'message_partner';\n+\n+COMMIT;\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Implement Partner Bridge paths while preserving existing bridge functionality and existing local distillation edits. Add a partner bridge path field to existing bridge candidates with values message_partner, coach_in_person, casual_share, hold_for_context, ask_permission, do_not_bridge. message_partner means do not proactively send now; it means keep the ready bridge in the target partner prompt/hot context until it is substantively addressed. Keep send_bridge_candidate as explicit immediate send behavior but clarify prompt semantics. Hot context should surface ready/actionable target-facing partner bridges only, support multiple ready bridges, and exclude sent/addressed by default. Prompt should use Partner Bridge terminology, resolve the escalate_to_partner conflict, require marking addressed only when substantively dealt with or target declines/no longer wants to discuss, and instruct continued discussion until addressed. {
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/tool_schemas.py\n@@\n class BridgeCandidateSensitivity(str, Enum):\n     low = \"low\"\n     medium = \"medium\"\n     high = \"high\"\n \n \n+class BridgeCandidatePartnerPath(str, Enum):\n+    \"\"\"How a bridge candidate should be handled across the partner bridge.\"\"\"\n+\n+    # Keep the ready row in the target partner prompt/hot context until it is\n+    # substantively addressed; this does not proactively send it now.\n+    message_partner = \"message_partner\"\n+    # Coach the source user to discuss the bridge in person.\n+    coach_in_person = \"coach_in_person\"\n+    # Suggest a casual source-side share without creating target prompt pressure.\n+    casual_share = \"casual_share\"\n+    # Hold as source-side context/bookkeeping until a better opening appears.\n+    hold_for_context = \"hold_for_context\"\n+    # Ask the source user for explicit permission before creating a target-facing bridge.\n+    ask_permission = \"ask_permission\"\n+    # Audit-only path: record that the material should not become a bridge.\n+    do_not_bridge = \"do_not_bridge\"\n+\n+\n class PerspectiveTemplate(str, Enum):\n     nvc = \"nvc\"\n     gottman = \"gottman\"\n     ifs_parts = \"ifs_parts\"\n     reflective_listener = \"reflective_listener\"\n@@\n     kind: BridgeCandidateKind\n     status: BridgeCandidateStatus\n     sensitivity: BridgeCandidateSensitivity\n+    partner_path: BridgeCandidatePartnerPath\n     source_message_ids: list[UUID]\n     related_memory_ids: list[UUID] = Field(default_factory=list)\n     related_observation_ids: list[UUID] = Field(default_factory=list)\n@@\n     target_user_id: UUID\n     kind: BridgeCandidateKind\n+    partner_path: BridgeCandidatePartnerPath = Field(\n+        description=\"Partner bridge path. Use message_partner for a ready target-facing bridge that stays in the target prompt until addressed; use do_not_bridge for audit-only non-bridges.\"\n+    )\n     sensitivity: BridgeCandidateSensitivity = BridgeCandidateSensitivity.medium\n     source_message_ids: list[UUID] = Field(min_length=1)\n     related_memory_ids: list[UUID] = Field(default_factory=list)\n@@\n     source_user_id: UUID | None = None\n     target_user_id: UUID | None = None\n     status: BridgeCandidateStatus | None = None\n+    partner_path: BridgeCandidatePartnerPath | None = None\n     limit: int = Field(default=10, ge=1, le=50)\n@@\n     kind: BridgeCandidateKind | None = None\n     status: BridgeCandidateStatus | None = None\n     sensitivity: BridgeCandidateSensitivity | None = None\n+    partner_path: BridgeCandidatePartnerPath | None = Field(\n+        default=None,\n+        description=\"Optional partner bridge path update.         Idea:
Implement Partner Bridge paths while preserving existing bridge functionality and existing local distillation edits. Add a partner bridge path field to existing bridge candidates with values message_partner, coach_in_person, casual_share, hold_for_context, ask_permission, do_not_bridge. message_partner means do not proactively send now; it means keep the ready bridge in the target partner prompt/hot context until it is substantively addressed. Keep send_bridge_candidate as explicit immediate send behavior but clarify prompt semantics. Hot context should surface ready/actionable target-facing partner bridges only, support multiple ready bridges, and exclude sent/addressed by default. Prompt should use Partner Bridge terminology, resolve the escalate_to_partner conflict, require marking addressed only when substantively dealt with or target declines/no longer wants to discuss, and instruct continued discussion until addressed. Add schema, migration, tool, hot context, prompt, and focused tests. Consider whether existing memories/observations/distillations need backfill: do not auto-create historical bridges in migration; document/manual/script path if needed.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/cross_thread_privacy.py\n@@\n def bridge_candidate_visible_to_target(\n     candidate: Mapping[str, Any],\n     *,\n     target_user_id: UUID | None = None,\n ) -> bool:\n-    if not is_bridge_status_target_visible(candidate.get(\"status\")):\n+    status = candidate.get(\"status\")\n+    if isinstance(status, Enum):\n+        status = status.value\n+    status = str(status)\n+    if not is_bridge_status_target_visible(status):\n         return False\n+    if status == \"ready\":\n+        partner_path = candidate.get(\"partner_path\", \"message_partner\")\n+        if isinstance(partner_path, Enum):\n+            partner_path = partner_path.value\n+        # Gate ready rows by path so source-only bookkeeping rows such as\n+        # hold_for_context or coach_in_person cannot leak through target lists.\n+        if partner_path != \"message_partner\":\n+            return False\n     if target_user_id is None:\n         return True\n     return candidate.get(\"target_user_id\") == target_user_id\n*** End Patch\n"

        Idea:
Implement Partner Bridge paths while preserving existing bridge functionality and existing local distillation edits. Add a partner bridge path field to existing bridge candidates with values message_partner, coach_in_person, casual_share, hold_for_context, ask_permission, do_not_bridge. message_partner means do not proactively send now; it means keep the ready bridge in the target partner prompt/hot context until it is substantively addressed. Keep send_bridge_candidate as explicit immediate send behavior but clarify prompt semantics. Hot context should surface ready/actionable target-facing partner bridges only, support multiple ready bridges, and exclude sent/addressed by default. Add schema, migration, tool, hot context, prompt, and focused tests. Consider whether existing memories/observations/distillations need backfill: do not auto-create historical bridges in migration; document/manual/script path if needed.

        Idea:
Implement Partner Bridge paths while preserving existing bridge functionality and existing local distillation edits. Add a partner bridge path field to existing bridge candidates with values message_partner, coach_in_person, casual_share, hold_for_context, ask_permission, do_not_bridge. message_partner means do not proactively send now; it means keep the ready bridge in the target partner prompt/hot context until it is substantively addressed. Keep send_bridge_candidate as explicit immediate send behavior but clarify prompt semantics. Hot context should surface ready/actionable target-facing partner bridges only, support multiple ready bridges, and exclude sent/addressed by default. Prompt should use Partner Bridge terminology, resolve the escalate_to_partner conflict, require marking addressed only when substantively dealt with or target declines/no longer wants to discuss, and instruct continued discussion until addressed. Add schema, migration, tool, hot context, prompt, and focused tests. Consider whether existing memories/observations/distillations need backfill: do not auto-create historical bridges in migration; document/manual/script path if needed.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/hot_context.py\n@@\n-        SELECT id, source_user_id, target_user_id, kind, status, sensitivity,\n+        SELECT id, source_user_id, target_user_id, kind, status, sensitivity, partner_path,\n                shareable_summary, created_at\n         FROM bridge_candidates\n         WHERE target_user_id=$1\n           AND source_user_id=$2\n-          AND status IN ('ready', 'sent', 'addressed')\n+          AND status='ready'\n+          AND partner_path='message_partner'\n         ORDER BY created_at DESC\n-        LIMIT 3\n+        LIMIT 5\n@@\n             \"kind\": row[\"kind\"],\n             \"status\": row[\"status\"],\n             \"sensitivity\": row[\"sensitivity\"],\n+            \"partner_path\": row[\"partner_path\"],\n             \"shareable_summary\": row[\"shareable_summary\"],\n         }\n@@\n     if hc.bridge_candidates:\n         lines.extend(\n-            f\"- id={_clip_id(item['id'], clip_limit)} kind={item['kind']} status={item['status']} sensitivity={item['sensitivity']} source={_clip_id(item['source_user_id'], clip_limit)}: {_clip(item['shareable_summary'], clip_limit)}\"\n+            f\"- id={_clip_id(item['id'], clip_limit)} kind={item['kind']} status={item['status']} partner_path={item['partner_path']} sensitivity={item['sensitivity']} source={_clip_id(item['source_user_id'], clip_limit)}: {_clip(item['shareable_summary'], clip_limit)}\"\n             for item in hc.bridge_candidates\n         )\n-        lines.append(\"- use list_bridge_candidates for older or filtered bridge candidates.\")\n+        lines.append(\"- use list_bridge_candidates for sent, addressed, or non-message_partner bridge candidates.\")\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Veas/app/services/prompts.py\n@@\n-SYSTEM_PROMPT_V2 = SYSTEM_PROMPT_V1\n+SYSTEM_PROMPT_V2 = (\n+    SYSTEM_PROMPT_V1\n+    .replace(\n+        \"\"\"# Bridge Candidates\n+\n+Use bridge candidates for cross-thread material that may help the other partner understand, repair, clarify, or contextualize something. This is the permission-aware bridge path; do not manually copy raw partner-private text into the other user's answer.\n+\n+Create a bridge candidate when one partner says something that materially explains, contradicts, clarifies, softens, or adds important context to something the other partner has said, and a shareable version may help. Link the source message ids when possible. Use `shareable_summary` for the neutral, non-inflammatory wording; keep private/raw reasoning in `internal_note`.\n+\n+Lifecycle: create as `pending` when the source user is opt-out or unset and hasn't authorized this specific bridge, mark `ready` when shareable, then send via `send_bridge_candidate` (which sends only the `shareable_summary` through the guarded outbound path). Sensitive material stays pending or blocked until safe. Full lifecycle states live in the bridge candidate tool descriptions.\"\"\",\n+        \"\"\"# Partner Bridges\n+\n+Use Partner Bridges for cross-thread material that may help the other partner understand, repair, clarify, or contextualize something. This is the permission-aware bridge path; do not manually copy raw partner-private text into the other user's answer.\n+\n+Create a bridge candidate when one partner says something that materially explains, contradicts, clarifies, softens, or adds important context to something the other partner has said, and a shareable version may help. Link the source message ids when possible. Use `shareable_summary` for the neutral, non-inflammatory wording; keep private/raw reasoning in `internal_note`.\n+\n+Set `partner_path` deliberately:\n+- `message_partner`: create a ready/actionable Partner Bridge for the target partner's prompt and hot context. Do not proactively call `send_bridge_candidate`; keep raising it at natural openings until the target substantively engages with it (`addressed`) or declines/no longer wants to discuss it (`declined`) via `update_bridge_candidate`.\n+- `coach_in_person`: help the source user bring it up directly in person.\n+- `casual_share`: suggest a low-pressure direct share by the source user.\n+- `hold_for_context`: keep it as source-side context for later; do not surface it to the target.\n+- `ask_permission`: ask the source user for clearer permission before making it target-facing.\n+- `do_not_bridge`: audit-only; do not bridge this material.\n+\n+`send_bridge_candidate` is the explicit immediate-send affordance for when the user wants the summary sent now. Sensitive material stays pending or blocked until safe. Full lifecycle states live in the bridge candidate tool descriptions.\"\"\",\n+    )\n+    .replace(\n+        \"If the user accepts a bridge offer or asks you to message/tell their partner, use `escalate_to_partner` with concise, balanced, non-accusatory wording, clearly marked as a mediated summary. Exclude protected OOB details, private analysis, pressure, threats, or anything designed to manage the partner's reaction.\",\n+        \"If the user accepts a bridge offer or asks you to message/tell their partner, create a bridge candidate, typically `partner_path=message_partner`, or use `send_bridge_candidate` when the user wants it sent now. `escalate_to_partner` remains restricted to the crisis gates. Exclude protected OOB details, private analysis, pressure, threats, or anything designed to manage the partner's reaction.\",\n+    )\n+)\n*** End Patch\n"
}
